    cashout:{book:['Up nicely. I am cashing out.'],hunch:['Quitting while I am ahead, bye!'],never:['Taking my winnings home. Night, folks.'],counter:['Coloring up.'],whale:['Cashing out, the drinks are on me!']}};
  var benchSince={};
  function isBot(s){return !!s&&typeof s.id==='string'&&s.id.indexOf('bot:')===0;}
  function botKey(s){var d=BOTBY[s.id];return d?d.sty:'book';}
  function pick(a){return a[Math.floor(Math.random()*a.length)];}
  function trueCount(){var left=Array.isArray(shoe)?shoe.length:0;return left?runCount/Math.max(0.5,left/52):0;}
  function humansHere(){return T?T.seats.filter(function(s){return !isBot(s)&&!s.gone;}).length:0;}
  function soloish(){return humansHere()<=1;}
  function chatPost(s,txt){try{var d=BOTBY[s.id];if(window.__bjChat&&window.__bjChat.post)window.__bjChat.post(s.id,s.name,txt,d?d.col:'');}catch(e){}}
  function say(s,txt,prob,delay){
    if(!TALK||!s||!txt||(prob!==undefined&&Math.random()>prob))return;
    setTimeout(function(){if(!on||!host||!T||(T.seats.indexOf(s)<0&&!s.leaving))return;s.say={t:txt,seq:++sayN};dirty();chatPost(s,txt);},delay||0);
  }
  function talk(s,ev,prob,delay){var p=LINES[ev];if(!p)return;var a=p[botKey(s)];if(a)say(s,pick(a),prob,delay);}
  function botBet(b){
    var k=botKey(b),x=25,tc=trueCount();
    if(k==='hunch')x=b.lastNet>0?Math.min(Math.max(b.bet||25,25)*2,200):pick([10,25,25,50]);
    else if(k==='never')x=50;
    else if(k==='whale')x=b.lastNet<0?Math.min((b.bet||100)*2,1000):pick([100,150,200,300]);
    else if(k==='counter'){x=25*(tc>=5?8:tc>=4?6:tc>=3?4:tc>=2?2:1);if(x>=100)say(b,'Feels like a good shoe.',0.3,600);}
    else if(b.key==='pearl')x=10;
    x=Math.max(R.min,Math.min(x,Math.floor(b.bank)));
    b.bet=x;b.sides={};
    if((k==='hunch'||k==='whale')&&activeSides.length&&Math.random()<0.5){var sa=k==='whale'?25:5;if(b.bank>=x+sa)b.sides[pick(activeSides)]=sa;}
    b.ready=true;
  }
  function sysChat(t){try{if(window.__bjChat&&window.__bjChat.sys)window.__bjChat.sys(t);}catch(e){}}
  function nextRegular(taken){
    var seated={};T.seats.forEach(function(x){seated[x.id]=1;});(taken||[]).forEach(function(x){seated[x.id]=1;});
    var free=BOTDEF.filter(function(d){return !seated[d.id];});if(!free.length)return null;
    free.sort(function(a,b){return (benchSince[a.id]||0)-(benchSince[b.id]||0);});
    var fresh=free.filter(function(d){return !benchSince[d.id];});
    var d=fresh.length?pick(fresh):pick(free.slice(0,Math.max(1,Math.ceil(free.length/2))));
    var b=newSeat(d.id,d.name,1000);return b;
  }
  function syncBots(){
    if(!T||running||T.phase!=='bet')return;
    var humans=T.seats.filter(function(s){return !isBot(s);}),want=Math.max(0,Math.min(BOTSN,MAXS-humans.length-T.pending.length));
    var first=-1;T.seats.forEach(function(x,i){if(first<0&&!isBot(x))first=i;});
    var right=[],left=[];T.seats.forEach(function(x,i){if(isBot(x))((first<0||i<first)?right:left).push(x);});
    var arrived=[],gone=[];
    /* broke regulars leave, big winners sometimes cash out, and someone new sits down in that chair */
    function swap(arr){arr.forEach(function(b,i){
      var broke=b.bank<R.min,cash=!broke&&b.bank>=2500&&b.st.hands>=6&&Math.random()<0.25;
      if(!broke&&!cash)return;
      b.leaving=true;talk(b,broke?'broke':'cashout',1,0);gone.push(b);benchSince[b.id]=Date.now();
      var n=nextRegular(right.concat(left));if(n){arr[i]=n;arrived.push(n);}else arr.splice(i,1);});}
    swap(right);swap(left);
    while(right.length+left.length>want){if(right.length>left.length)right.pop();else left.pop();}
    while(right.length+left.length<want){var n2=nextRegular(right.concat(left));if(!n2)break;if(right.length<=left.length)right.push(n2);else left.push(n2);arrived.push(n2);}
    T.seats=right.concat(humans,left);
    T.seats.forEach(function(b){if(isBot(b)&&(!b.ready||b.bet<R.min))botBet(b);});
    var initial=T.round<1&&!gone.length;
    gone.forEach(function(b,i){setTimeout(function(){sysChat(b.name+' left the table');},300+i*200);});
    arrived.forEach(function(b,i){if(!initial||arrived.length<=2){setTimeout(function(){sysChat(b.name+' sat down');},gone.length?1500:200);talk(b,'hello',initial?0.7:0.85,(gone.length?2200:700)+i*900);}});
    if(gone.length||arrived.length)setTimeout(function(){try{liveUI();}catch(e){}},50);
  }
  function botDecide(s,h,lg){
    var k=botKey(s),up=T.dealer[0],x=total(h.cards),uv=val(up),base=strategyFor(h,up,lg);
    if(k==='never'){
      if(lg.split&&(h.cards[0].r===1||val(h.cards[0])===8))return 'split';
      if(lg.double&&!x.soft&&x.t===11)return 'double';
      if(x.soft)return x.t>=18?'stand':'hit';
      return x.t>=12?'stand':'hit';
    }
    if(k==='whale'&&Math.random()<0.3&&lg.double&&!x.soft&&x.t>=8&&x.t<=12)return 'double';
    if(s.id==='bot:rusty'&&!x.soft&&x.t===16&&base!=='surrender')return 'hit';
    if((k==='hunch'||k==='whale')&&Math.random()<(k==='hunch'?0.25:0.1)){
      if(base==='stand'&&!x.soft&&x.t<=16){say(s,pick(['Feeling lucky. Hit me!','One more, come on!']),0.7);return 'hit';}
      if(base==='hit'&&lg.double&&x.t>=8&&x.t<=12)return 'double';
      if(lg.split&&val(h.cards[0])===10)return 'split';
    }
    if(k==='counter'){var tc=trueCount();
      if(!x.soft&&x.t===16&&uv===10&&tc>0)return 'stand';
      if(!x.soft&&x.t===12&&(uv===2||uv===3)&&tc>=2)return 'stand';
      if(lg.split&&val(h.cards[0])===10&&(uv===5||uv===6)&&tc>=5)return 'split';
    }
    return base;
  }
  async function botAct(s,h){
    var lg=legalFor(s,h),k=botDecide(s,h,lg),x=total(h.cards);
    await later(((k!=='hit'&&k!=='stand')||(x.t>=12&&x.t<=16)?950:600)+Math.random()*650);
    return k;
  }
  function botIns(s){var k=botKey(s);if(k==='never')return true;if(k==='counter')return trueCount()>=3;if(k==='hunch'||k==='whale')return Math.random()<0.3;return false;}
  function botList(){return T?T.seats.filter(isBot).map(function(b){var d=BOTBY[b.id];return {id:b.id,name:b.name,key:d.sty,col:d.col,tag:d.tag};}):[];}
  function onChat(m,local){
    if(!on||!host||!T||!TALK)return;var bots=botList();if(!bots.length)return;
    Live.banter(m.text,bots,{game:'blackjack',
      advice:function(){var st=lastState,mi=-1;if(!st||!st.cur)return '';st.seats.forEach(function(x,i){if(x.id===m.from)mi=i;});
        if(mi<0||st.cur.seat!==mi||st.phase!=='play')return 'Wait till you have your cards, then ask me.';
        var seat=T.seats[mi],h=seat&&seat.hands[st.cur.hand];if(!h)return '';var mv=strategyFor(h,T.dealer[0],legalFor(seat,h));
        var up=T.dealer[0].r===1?'an ace':'a '+val(T.dealer[0]);return 'Book says '+mv+' on '+totalText(h.cards)+' against '+up+'.';},
      mood:function(b){var sx=seatOf(b.id);if(!sx)return 'even';return sx.bank>1050?'up':sx.bank<950?'down':'even';},
      say:function(b,txt,delay){var sx=seatOf(b.id);if(sx)say(sx,txt,undefined,delay);}});
  }
  function isOn(){return on;}
  function me(){return L?L.me:{id:'local',name:'You'};}
  function hostName(){var h=roster.filter(function(p){return p.isHost;})[0];return h?h.name:'the host';}

  /* ===== host engine ===== */
  function newSeat(id,name,bank){return {id:id,name:name,bank:bank,bet:0,sides:{},ready:false,hands:[],init:null,live:[],insurance:0,insDecided:true,committed:0,gone:false,note:'',noteBad:false,inRound:false,st:{w:0,l:0,p:0,bj:0,dw:0,big:0,side:0,hands:0}};}
  function seatOf(id){if(!T)return null;for(var i=0;i<T.seats.length;i++)if(T.seats[i].id===id)return T.seats[i];return null;}
  function seatIdx(id){if(!T)return -1;for(var i=0;i<T.seats.length;i++)if(T.seats[i].id===id)return i;return -1;}
  function syncSeats(){
    if(!T)return;
    roster.forEach(function(p){var s=seatOf(p.id);if(s){s.name=p.name;s.gone=false;}else if(T.phase==='bet'&&!running)T.seats.push(newSeat(p.id,p.name,1000));else if(T.pending.indexOf(p.id)<0)T.pending.push(p.id);});
    T.seats.forEach(function(s){s.gone=!isBot(s)&&s.id!==me().id&&!roster.some(function(p){return p.id===s.id;});});
    if(T.phase==='bet'&&!running){
      T.seats=T.seats.filter(function(s){return !s.gone;});
      T.pending.forEach(function(id){var p=roster.filter(function(x){return x.id===id;})[0];if(p&&!seatOf(id))T.seats.push(newSeat(p.id,p.name,1000));});T.pending=[];
      syncBots();
    }
    if(waiter&&T.cur){var cs=T.seats[T.cur.seat];if(cs&&cs.gone)resolveWait('stand');}
    if(insWait){T.seats.forEach(function(s){if(s.gone&&s.inRound&&!s.insDecided){s.insDecided=true;}});checkIns();}
    dirty();
  }
  function enterHost(fromState){
    on=true;host=true;myReady=false;
    T={seats:[],phase:'bet',dealer:[],holeDown:false,cur:null,deadline:0,readyAt:0,pending:[],tableCards:0,others:[],round:0,dealerTxt:''};
    if(fromState&&fromState.seats){
      /* the old host left: keep everyone's money, hand back anything bet on the unfinished round */
      fromState.seats.forEach(function(o){var s=newSeat(o.id,o.name,o.bank+(fromState.phase!=='bet'?(o.committed||0):0));s.bet=o.bet||0;s.sides=o.sides||{};s.st=o.st||s.st;T.seats.push(s);});
      if(fromState.phase!=='bet')toast('The host left. Bets on that hand were returned and the new host shuffles a fresh shoe.');
      buildShoe();firstShoe=false;
    }else{var m0=newSeat(me().id,me().name,bank);m0.st=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ST));T.seats.push(m0);}
    var mine=seatOf(me().id);if(!mine){mine=newSeat(me().id,me().name,1000);T.seats.unshift(mine);}
    syncSeats();$('felt').classList.add('mp');dirty();
    clearInterval(beatT);beatT=setInterval(function(){if(host)cast();},2500);
  }
  function leaveToSolo(){
    var mine=lastState&&lastState.seats?lastState.seats.filter(function(s){return s.id===me().id;})[0]:null;
    on=false;host=false;T=null;running=false;myReady=false;clearInterval(beatT);
    if(waiter){var w=waiter;waiter=null;w('stand');}if(insWait){var iw=insWait;insWait=null;iw();}
    if(mine)bank=mine.bank;
    phase='bet';busy=false;hands=[];dealer=[];holeEl=null;live=[];active=0;
    $('dealerCards').innerHTML='';$('hands').innerHTML='';$('othersL').innerHTML='';$('othersR').innerHTML='';pods={};drawn={dealer:'',mine:'',others:''};$('sideStrip').innerHTML='';$('dealerTotal').classList.add('hide');
    $('felt').classList.remove('mp');hideViewShuf();
    if(!Array.isArray(shoe)||!shoe.length||!totalCards){shoe=[];buildShoe();}
    lockRules(false);rulesText();syncRuleUI();renderSideMenu();shoeHud();hud();setButtons();liveUI();
  }
  function startCountdown(){if(!T.readyAt){T.readyAt=Date.now()+READY_MS;setTimeout(maybeDeal,READY_MS+50);}}
  function maybeDeal(){
    if(!host||!T||running||T.phase!=='bet')return;
    var bettors=T.seats.filter(function(s){return !s.gone&&s.bet>=R.min;}),ready=bettors.filter(function(s){return s.ready;});
    if(!ready.length){T.readyAt=0;dirty();return;}
    var present=T.seats.filter(function(s){return !s.gone;});
    if(ready.length===present.length||(T.readyAt&&Date.now()>=T.readyAt)){runRound();return;}
    startCountdown();dirty();
  }
  function legalFor(s,h){
    if(!h||h.done)return {double:false,split:false,surrender:false};
    var x=total(h.cards),two=h.cards.length===2,dbl=(R.sp?(h.cards.length>=2&&!h.doubled&&x.t<21):two)&&!h.aces&&s.bank>=h.bet&&(!h.fromSplit||R.das);
    if(!R.sp){if(dbl&&R.dbl==='9-11')dbl=!x.soft&&x.t>=9&&x.t<=11;if(dbl&&R.dbl==='10-11')dbl=!x.soft&&x.t>=10&&x.t<=11;}
    return {double:dbl,split:two&&val(h.cards[0])===val(h.cards[1])&&s.hands.length<R.maxHands&&s.bank>=h.bet&&!h.aces,
      surrender:R.surrender&&two&&s.hands.length===1&&!h.fromSplit};
  }
  function waitFor(ms,dflt){return new Promise(function(res){var t=setTimeout(function(){if(waiter===fin)fin(dflt);},ms);function fin(v){clearTimeout(t);if(waiter===fin)waiter=null;res(v);}waiter=fin;});}
  function resolveWait(v){if(waiter)waiter(v);}
  function checkIns(){if(!insWait||!T)return;if(T.seats.every(function(s){return !s.inRound||s.insDecided;})){var w=insWait;insWait=null;w();}}
  function dealOne(cards){var c=drawCard();cards.push(c);runCount+=hilo(c);SFX.card();return c;}
  function sideCtxFor(s){var dx=total(T.dealer).t;return {p1:s.init.p1,p2:s.init.p2,up:s.init.up,dbj:T.dealer.length===2&&dx===21,dbust:dx>21,dn:T.dealer.length,pbj:s.init.pbj};}
  function resolveSidesFor(s,stage){
    if(!s.live.length||!s.init)return;var ctx=sideCtxFor(s);
    s.live.forEach(function(b){if(b.res)return;var d=sideDef(b.id);if(!d)return;var w=d.when;
      if(!(stage==='end'||(stage==='initial'&&w==='initial')||(stage==='peek'&&w==='peek')))return;
      var r=d.eval(ctx);s.committed-=b.amt;
      if(r){var pay=b.amt*r[0];s.bank+=b.amt+pay;b.res={win:true,txt:r[1]+' +'+money(pay),pay:pay};s.st.side+=pay;}
      else{b.res={win:false,txt:'Lost',pay:-b.amt};s.st.side-=b.amt;}});
  }
  async function runRound(){
    if(running)return;running=true;busy=true;
    var players=T.seats.filter(function(s){return !s.gone&&s.ready&&s.bet>=R.min;});
    players.forEach(function(s){var st=0;for(var k in s.sides){if(activeSides.indexOf(k)<0||!(s.sides[k]>0))delete s.sides[k];else st+=s.sides[k];}if(s.bank<s.bet+st){s.sides={};if(s.bank<s.bet){s.ready=false;}}});
    players=players.filter(function(s){return s.ready;});
    if(!players.length){running=false;busy=false;T.readyAt=0;dirty();return;}
    T.seats.forEach(function(s){s.hands=[];s.live=[];s.note='';s.inRound=false;s.insurance=0;s.insDecided=true;});
    discards+=T.tableCards;T.tableCards=0;T.dealer=[];T.holeDown=false;T.cur=null;T.readyAt=0;T.others=[];
    T.phase='deal';dirty();
    if(cutReached||shoe.length<=cutAt||firstShoe){
      if(R.cine){cast();Ceremony.autoCutMs=soloish()?0:15000;await Ceremony.run(firstShoe);Ceremony.autoCutMs=0;cast('shufdone');}
      else{buildShoe();}
      if(!firstShoe)shoesPlayed++;firstShoe=false;
    }
    T.round++;
    players.forEach(function(s){var st=0;for(var k in s.sides)st+=s.sides[k];s.bank-=s.bet+st;s.committed=s.bet+st;s.inRound=true;s.ready=false;s.st.hands++;
      s.hands=[{cards:[],bet:s.bet,doubled:false,done:false,fromSplit:false,result:null}];
      s.live=Object.keys(s.sides).map(function(k){return {id:k,amt:s.sides[k],res:null};});});
    var ms0=seatOf(me().id);if(ms0&&ms0.inRound){lastBet=ms0.bet;lastSides=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ms0.sides));saveSettings();}
    for(var rd=0;rd<2;rd++){
      for(var i=0;i<players.length;i++){dealOne(players[i].hands[0].cards);dirty();await later(260);}
      if(rd===0||R.hole!=='enhc'){dealOne(T.dealer);if(rd===1){T.holeDown=true;runCount-=hilo(T.dealer[1]);}dirty();await later(260);}
    }
    var up=T.dealer[0],dBJ=R.hole!=='enhc'&&total(T.dealer).t===21;
    players.forEach(function(s){var h=s.hands[0];s.init={p1:h.cards[0],p2:h.cards[1],up:up,pbj:isBJ(h)};resolveSidesFor(s,'initial');});
    (function(){var dl=0;players.forEach(function(s){if(!s.init.pbj)return;if(isBot(s)){talk(s,'bj',0.75,dl);dl+=900;}
      else{var bs=players.filter(isBot);if(bs.length)talk(pick(bs),'nicebj',0.6,dl+400);if(s.id===me().id)SFX.bj();}});})();
    dirty();await later(400);
    if(up.r===1){
      var insMs=soloish()?FOREVER:INS_MS;
      T.phase='insurance';T.deadline=Date.now()+insMs;players.forEach(function(s){s.insDecided=s.gone;});
      players.forEach(function(s){if(isBot(s)){var y=botIns(s)&&s.bank>=s.hands[0].bet/2;applyIns(s,y);if(y)talk(s,'ins',0.5,300);}});dirty();
      if(away){var mz=seatOf(me().id);if(mz&&mz.inRound&&!mz.insDecided)applyIns(mz,false);}
      await new Promise(function(res){insWait=res;setTimeout(function(){if(insWait===res){insWait=null;res();}},insMs);checkIns();});
      players.forEach(function(s){s.insDecided=true;});
    }
    if(R.hole!=='enhc'){
      if(up.r===1||val(up)===10){T.phase='peek';dirty();await later(650);}
      settleInsurance(players,dBJ);
      players.forEach(function(s){resolveSidesFor(s,'peek');});
      if(dBJ){T.holeDown=false;runCount+=hilo(T.dealer[1]);dirty();await later(450);await settleAll(players);return;}
    }
    /* each seat plays in turn */
    T.phase='play';
    for(var si=0;si<players.length;si++){
      var s=players[si],sIdx=T.seats.indexOf(s);
      for(var hi=0;hi<s.hands.length;hi++){
        var h=s.hands[hi];
        if(h.cards.length<2){dealOne(h.cards);dirty();await later(260);}
        if(h.aces||isBJ(h)||total(h.cards).t>=21){h.done=true;continue;}
        while(!h.done){
          var turnMs=isBot(s)?0:soloish()?FOREVER:TURN_MS;
          T.cur={seat:sIdx,hand:hi,legal:legalFor(s,h)};T.deadline=Date.now()+turnMs;dirty();
          var k=(s.gone||(away&&s.id===me().id))?'stand':isBot(s)?await botAct(s,h):await waitFor(turnMs,'stand');
          var L2=legalFor(s,h);
          if(k==='double'&&!L2.double)k='hit';if(k==='split'&&!L2.split)k='hit';if(k==='surrender'&&!L2.surrender)k='stand';
          if(isBot(s)&&(k==='double'||k==='split'))talk(s,k,0.45);
          if(k==='hit'){dealOne(h.cards);var t=total(h.cards).t;if(t>21){h.done=true;h.result={cls:'lose',txt:'Bust'};if(isBot(s))talk(s,'bust',0.5,250);}else if(t===21)h.done=true;}
          else if(k==='stand'){h.done=true;}
          else if(k==='double'){s.bank-=h.bet;s.committed+=h.bet;h.bet*=2;h.doubled=true;dealOne(h.cards);h.done=true;if(total(h.cards).t>21)h.result={cls:'lose',txt:'Bust'};}
          else if(k==='split'){s.bank-=h.bet;s.committed+=h.bet;var moved=h.cards.pop(),aces=h.cards[0].r===1&&!R.sp;h.fromSplit=true;h.aces=aces;
            s.hands.splice(hi+1,0,{cards:[moved],bet:h.bet,doubled:false,done:false,fromSplit:true,aces:aces,result:null});dealOne(h.cards);
            if(aces||total(h.cards).t===21)h.done=true;}
          else if(k==='surrender'){h.done=true;h.surrendered=true;h.result={cls:'push',txt:'Surrendered'};s.bank+=h.bet/2;s.committed-=h.bet/2;}
          dirty();await later(220);
        }
      }
    }
    T.cur=null;
    /* dealer */
    T.phase='dealer';T.holeDown=false;if(T.dealer[1])runCount+=hilo(T.dealer[1]);dirty();await later(450);
    if(R.hole==='enhc'){dealOne(T.dealer);dBJ=total(T.dealer).t===21;settleInsurance(players,dBJ);players.forEach(function(s){resolveSidesFor(s,'peek');});dirty();await later(400);}
    if(!dBJ){
      var liveHand=players.some(function(s){return s.hands.some(function(h){return !h.surrendered&&total(h.cards).t<=21&&!isBJ(h);})||s.live.some(function(b){return !b.res&&sideDef(b.id).when==='end';});});
      if(liveHand)while(true){var x=total(T.dealer);if(x.t>17||(x.t===17&&(!x.soft||R.s17)))break;dealOne(T.dealer);dirty();await later(420);}
    }
    await settleAll(players);
  }
  function settleInsurance(players,dBJ){
    players.forEach(function(s){if(!s.insurance)return;s.committed-=s.insurance;if(dBJ)s.bank+=s.insurance*3;});
  }
  async function settleAll(players){
    T.phase='settle';var dx=total(T.dealer).t,dBJ=T.dealer.length===2&&dx===21,dtxt=dx>21?'Dealer busts with '+dx+'.':dBJ?'Dealer has blackjack.':'Dealer has '+dx+'.',lines=[];
    players.forEach(function(s){
      var net=0;
      s.hands.forEach(function(h){
        var o=handOutcome(h,dx,dBJ);net+=o.net;s.st[o.k]++;if(o.bj)s.st.bj++;if(o.k==='w'&&h.doubled)s.st.dw++;
        if(o.bonus&&isBot(s))say(s,pick(['Bonus! '+o.bonus.txt+'!','Look at that, '+o.bonus.txt+'!']),0.8,300);
        s.bank+=o.pay;s.committed-=h.surrendered?h.bet/2:h.bet;h.result=o.res;h.done=true;
      });
      resolveSidesFor(s,'end');
      var insNet=s.insurance?(dBJ?s.insurance*2:-s.insurance):0,sideNet=0;s.live.forEach(function(b){if(b.res)sideNet+=b.res.pay||0;});
      var all=net+insNet+sideNet,parts=[];s.committed=0;
      if(s.live.length||s.insurance){parts.push('Main bet '+sg(net));if(s.insurance)parts.push('Insurance '+sg(insNet));if(s.live.length)parts.push('Side bets '+sg(sideNet));}
      var brk=parts.length?'<br><span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:var(--muted)">'+parts.join(' &middot; ')+'</span>':'';
      s.note=dtxt+(all>0?' You win <b>'+money(all)+'</b>'+(parts.length?' total':'')+'.':all<0?' You lose <b>'+money(-all)+'</b>'+(parts.length?' net':'')+'.':(parts.length?' <b>Even</b> on the hand.':' <b>Push.</b> Your bet comes back.'))+brk;
      s.noteBad=all<0;s.lastNet=all;if(all>s.st.big)s.st.big=all;
      lines.push({id:s.id,name:s.name,net:all});
      if(s.id===me().id){if(all>0)SFX.win();else if(all<0)SFX.lose();}
    });
    (function(){var bs=players.filter(isBot);if(!bs.length)return;
      if(dx>21&&!dBJ){var hk=bs.filter(function(b){return botKey(b)==='never';})[0];talk(hk||pick(bs),'dbust',0.55,500);return;}
      var b=pick(bs);if(b.lastNet>=100)talk(b,'win',0.6,500);else if(b.lastNet>0)talk(b,'win',0.2,500);else if(b.lastNet<0)talk(b,'lose',0.22,500);})();
    T.others=lines;
    T.tableCards=T.dealer.length+players.reduce(function(n,s){return n+s.hands.reduce(function(m,h){return m+h.cards.length;},0);},0);
    T.phase='bet';T.cur=null;
    players.forEach(function(s){s.inRound=false;var st=0;for(var k in s.sides)st+=s.sides[k];if(s.bank<s.bet+st){s.sides={};if(s.bank<s.bet)s.bet=s.bank>=R.min?Math.floor(s.bank):0;}});
    running=false;busy=false;
    var mine=seatOf(me().id);if(mine){myReady=false;var a=0;for(var k2 in sideBets)a+=sideBets[k2];if(mine.bank<bet+a){sideBets={};if(mine.bank<bet)bet=mine.bank>=R.min?Math.floor(mine.bank):0;}}
    if(cutReached)setTimeout(function(){toast('Shuffling a fresh shoe before the next hand');},900);
    syncSeats();dirty();
    if(!wantOn)setTimeout(function(){if(T&&!running&&T.phase==='bet')leaveToSolo();},800);
  }
  function sg(n){return (n>0?'+':'')+money(n);}
  function esc(t){return String(t).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});}

  /* ===== state: what everyone draws from ===== */
  function snapshot(){
    var ds=T.dealer.map(function(c,i){return (i===1&&T.holeDown)?{d:1,id:c.id}:{s:c.s,r:c.r,id:c.id};}),shown=T.holeDown?[T.dealer[0]]:T.dealer;
    var dt='';if(T.dealer.length){var x=total(shown);dt=T.holeDown?totalText(shown):(T.dealer.length===2&&x.t===21?'Blackjack':totalText(shown));}
    return {v:++seq,host:me().id,hostName:me().name,phase:T.phase,round:T.round,
      rules:{sp:R.sp,decks:R.decks,s17:R.s17,pay:R.pay,dbl:R.dbl,das:R.das,surrender:R.surrender,hole:R.hole,maxHands:R.maxHands,preset:R.preset},sides:activeSides.slice(),
      dealer:ds,dealerTxt:dt,dealerBust:!T.holeDown&&total(T.dealer).t>21,dealerBJ:!T.holeDown&&T.dealer.length===2&&total(T.dealer).t===21,
      shoe:{left:shoe.length,total:totalCards,cutAt:cutAt,discards:discards,cutReached:cutReached,rc:runCount,shoes:shoesPlayed},
      cur:T.cur?{seat:T.cur.seat,hand:T.cur.hand,legal:T.cur.legal}:null,ms:T.cur||T.phase==='insurance'?Math.max(0,T.deadline-Date.now()):0,
      readyMs:T.readyAt?Math.max(0,T.readyAt-Date.now()):0,others:T.others,
      solo:soloish(),
      seats:T.seats.map(function(s){return {bot:isBot(s)?botKey(s):'',say:s.say||null,lastNet:s.lastNet||0,id:s.id,name:s.name,bank:s.bank,bet:s.bet,sides:s.sides,ready:s.ready,inRound:s.inRound,gone:s.gone,insurance:s.insurance,insDecided:s.insDecided,committed:s.committed,note:s.note,noteBad:s.noteBad,st:s.st,
        hands:s.hands.map(function(h){return {cards:h.cards.map(function(c){return {s:c.s,r:c.r,id:c.id};}),bet:h.bet,doubled:h.doubled,done:h.done,fromSplit:h.fromSplit,aces:h.aces,surrendered:h.surrendered,result:h.result};}),
        live:s.live.map(function(b){return {id:b.id,amt:b.amt,res:b.res};})};})};
  }
  function dirty(){if(!on||!host)return;clearTimeout(castT);castT=setTimeout(function(){cast();},60);}
  function cast(extra){
    if(!on||!host||!T)return;
    var st=snapshot();lastState=st;var net=L&&roster.length>=2;if(net)L.send('state',st);
    if(extra==='shufdone'&&net)L.send('shufdone',{});
    render(st);
  }

  /* ===== drawing (everyone) ===== */
  var drawn={dealer:'',mine:'',others:''};
  function cardEl(c){var el=c.d?makeCard({s:'s',r:2,id:c.id},true):makeCard(c,false);if(c.d)el.removeAttribute('data-id');return el;}
  function render(st){
    if(!on||!st)return;lastState=st;rcvAt=Date.now();
    var mid=me().id,mine=st.seats.filter(function(s){return s.id===mid;})[0]||null,meIdx=-1;st.seats.forEach(function(s,i){if(s.id===mid)meIdx=i;});
    if(!host){
      for(var k in st.rules)R[k]=st.rules[k];
      activeSides=st.sides.slice();for(var sk in sideBets)if(activeSides.indexOf(sk)<0)delete sideBets[sk];
      shoe={length:st.shoe.left};totalCards=st.shoe.total;cutAt=st.shoe.cutAt;discards=st.shoe.discards;
      if(cutReached!==st.shoe.cutReached){cutReached=st.shoe.cutReached;if(!cutReached)cutPopped=false;}
      runCount=st.shoe.rc;shoesPlayed=st.shoe.shoes;rulesText();shoeHud();
      if(viewShuf&&st.phase!=='deal')hideViewShuf();
    }
    $('felt').classList.add('mp');
    /* globals mirror my seat so the existing hint, chips and tooltips keep working */
    phase=st.phase==='peek'||st.phase==='dealer'||st.phase==='deal'?st.phase:st.phase;
    dealer=st.dealer.filter(function(c){return !c.d;});holeEl=st.dealer.some(function(c){return c.d;})?{}:null;
    if(mine){bank=mine.bank;hands=mine.hands;ST.w=mine.st.w;ST.l=mine.st.l;ST.p=mine.st.p;ST.bj=mine.st.bj;ST.dw=mine.st.dw;ST.big=mine.st.big;ST.side=mine.st.side;ST.hands=mine.st.hands;
      live=mine.live.map(function(b){return {def:sideDef(b.id),amt:b.amt,done:!!b.res,res:b.res};}).filter(function(b){return b.def;});}
    else{hands=[];live=[];}
    if(st.phase!=='bet')myReady=false;
    var myTurn=st.cur&&meIdx>=0&&st.cur.seat===meIdx;active=myTurn?st.cur.hand:-1;
    busy=st.phase==='bet'?myReady:!myTurn;
    /* dealer */
    var dk=st.dealer.map(function(c){return c.d?'x'+c.id:c.id;}).join(',');
    if(dk!==drawn.dealer){var box=$('dealerCards'),prev=drawn.dealer?drawn.dealer.split(','):[];
      if(!st.dealer.length||prev.length>st.dealer.length||prev.some(function(p,i){var c=st.dealer[i];return !c||(p.charAt(0)==='x'?!c.d||'x'+c.id!==p:String(c.id)!==p);})){
        if(prev.length&&st.dealer.length&&prev.length<=st.dealer.length){/* hole turned over: flip in place */
          var els=box.querySelectorAll('.card');st.dealer.forEach(function(c,i){if(i<els.length&&els[i].classList.contains('down')&&!c.d){var n=cardEl(c);n.classList.add('down');box.replaceChild(n,els[i]);void n.offsetWidth;n.classList.remove('down');}});
          for(var j=els.length;j<st.dealer.length;j++){var e2=cardEl(st.dealer[j]);box.appendChild(e2);flyFromShoe(e2);}
        }else{box.innerHTML='';st.dealer.forEach(function(c){box.appendChild(cardEl(c));});}
      }else{for(var i2=prev.length;i2<st.dealer.length;i2++){var e3=cardEl(st.dealer[i2]);box.appendChild(e3);flyFromShoe(e3);if(st.phase!=='bet')SFX.card();}}
      drawn.dealer=dk;}
    var t=$('dealerTotal');if(!st.dealer.length)t.classList.add('hide');else{t.classList.remove('hide');t.textContent=st.dealerTxt;t.className='total'+(st.dealerBust?' bust':'')+(st.dealerBJ?' bj':'');}
    /* my hands */
    var mk=mine?JSON.stringify(mine.hands.map(function(h){return [h.cards.map(function(c){return c.id;}),h.bet,h.result&&h.result.txt,h.doubled];}))+'|'+(myTurn?st.cur.hand:-1)+'|'+st.phase:'';
    if(mk!==drawn.mine){
      var hb=$('hands'),old=hb.querySelectorAll('.hand'),sameShape=mine&&old.length===mine.hands.length&&mine.hands.length>0;
      if(sameShape){mine.hands.forEach(function(h,i){var cs=old[i].querySelector('.cards'),have=cs.querySelectorAll('.card').length;
          if(have>h.cards.length){cs.innerHTML='';have=0;}
          for(var c=have;c<h.cards.length;c++){var el=cardEl(h.cards[c]);if(h.doubled&&c===2){el.classList.add('dbl');el.style.transform='rotate(90deg)';}cs.appendChild(el);if(drawn.mine)flyFromShoe(el);}});}
      else{hb.innerHTML='';if(mine)mine.hands.forEach(function(h){var d=document.createElement('div');d.className='hand';d.innerHTML='<div class="cards"></div><div class="meta"><span class="total"></span><span class="chipTag"></span></div>';
          var cs=d.querySelector('.cards');h.cards.forEach(function(c,ci){var el=cardEl(c);if(h.doubled&&ci===2){el.classList.add('dbl');el.style.transform='rotate(90deg)';}cs.appendChild(el);});hb.appendChild(d);});}
      handEls=[].slice.call(hb.querySelectorAll('.hand'));
      if(mine)mine.hands.forEach(function(h,i){updateHand(i);});
      drawn.mine=mk;}
    /* the other seats, around the curve of the table: first base on the right, third base on the left */
    var before=[],after=[];
    if(meIdx>=0)st.seats.forEach(function(x,i){if(i<meIdx)before.push(i);else if(i>meIdx)after.push(i);});
    else{var all0=st.seats.map(function(x,i){return i;}),hh0=Math.ceil(all0.length/2);before=all0.slice(0,hh0);after=all0.slice(hh0);}
    var keep={};
    function fill(box,list,side){
      var order=list.slice().reverse();
      order.forEach(function(i,j){var x=st.seats[i],p=podFor(x);keep[x.id]=1;
        var d=side==='R'?j+1:order.length-j;p.el.style.marginTop='calc(var(--arc,26px)*'+Math.max(0,2-d)+')';
        updatePod(p,x,i,st);if(box.children[j]!==p.el)box.insertBefore(p.el,box.children[j]||null);});
      while(box.children.length>order.length)box.removeChild(box.lastChild);
    }
    fill($('othersR'),before,'R');fill($('othersL'),after,'L');
    for(var pid in pods)if(!keep[pid]){if(pods[pid].el.parentNode)pods[pid].el.parentNode.removeChild(pods[pid].el);delete pods[pid];}
    var here=st.seats.filter(function(x){return !x.gone&&!x.bot;}).length;$('liveChip').classList.toggle('hide',here<2);$('liveChip').textContent='LIVE \u00b7 '+here+' players';
    if(!host)lockRules(true);
    renderStrip();hud();setButtons();liveUI();
  }
  var pods={};
  function hueCol(id){var h=0;for(var i=0;i<id.length;i++)h=(h*31+id.charCodeAt(i))>>>0;return 'hsl('+(h%360)+',70%,62%)';}
  function podFor(x){
    var p=pods[x.id];if(p)return p;
    var d=document.createElement('div');d.className='oseat';d.setAttribute('data-seat',x.id);
    d.innerHTML='<div class="talk"></div><div class="ohs"></div><div class="obet z"></div><div class="plate"><div class="av"></div><div class="pi"><div class="nm"></div><div class="ch"></div></div></div>';
    p={el:d,talk:d.querySelector('.talk'),ohs:d.querySelector('.ohs'),bet:d.querySelector('.obet'),av:d.querySelector('.av'),nm:d.querySelector('.nm'),ch:d.querySelector('.ch'),sig:'',bsig:'',nsig:'',talkSeq:-1,n:0,tt:0};
    var bd=BOTBY[x.id];if(bd)d.title=bd.name+' ('+bd.tag+'): '+bd.style;
    pods[x.id]=p;return p;
  }
  function updatePod(p,x,i,st){
    var turn=st.cur&&st.cur.seat===i&&st.phase==='play',won=st.phase==='bet'&&x.lastNet>0&&x.hands.length>0;
    p.el.className='oseat'+(turn?' turn':'')+(x.gone?' gone':'')+(won?' won':'');
    var bd=BOTBY[x.id],col=bd?bd.col:hueCol(x.id),nsig=x.name+'|'+col+'|'+(x.id===st.host);
    if(nsig!==p.nsig){p.nsig=nsig;p.av.style.background=col;p.el.style.setProperty('--pc',col);p.av.innerHTML=HAT+'<span>'+esc((x.name||'?').charAt(0).toUpperCase())+'</span>';
      p.nm.innerHTML=esc(x.name)+(!x.bot&&x.id===st.host&&!st.solo?'<i title="Host">&#9733;</i>':'');}
    p.ch.textContent=cmoney(x.bank);
    var amt=x.inRound?x.hands.reduce(function(a,h){return a+h.bet;},0):x.bet,sideAmt=0;for(var sk in (x.sides||{}))sideAmt+=x.sides[sk]||0;
    var showBet=amt>0&&(st.phase==='bet'||x.inRound),bsig=showBet+'|'+amt+'|'+sideAmt+'|'+(st.phase==='bet'&&!st.solo&&!x.bot&&x.ready);
    if(bsig!==p.bsig){p.bsig=bsig;p.bet.classList.toggle('z',!showBet);
      p.bet.innerHTML='<span class="stk"><i class="chipi n"></i><i class="chipi g"></i><i class="chipi"></i></span><span>'+cmoney(amt)+'</span>'+(sideAmt?'<small>+'+cmoney(sideAmt)+'</small>':'')+(st.phase==='bet'&&!st.solo&&!x.bot&&x.ready?'<small>READY</small>':'');}
    var n=0,sig=JSON.stringify(x.hands.map(function(h){n+=h.cards.length;return [h.cards.map(function(c){return c.id;}),h.result&&h.result.txt,h.doubled];}))+'|'+(turn?st.cur.hand:-1)+'|'+(turn&&x.bot);
    if(sig!==p.sig){p.sig=sig;
      p.ohs.innerHTML=x.hands.map(function(h,hi){var x2=total(h.cards),bj=h.cards.length===2&&x2.t===21&&!h.fromSplit,tt=h.cards.length?(bj?'BJ':totalText(h.cards)):'';
        return '<div class="oh'+(turn&&st.cur.hand===hi&&x.hands.length>1?' cur':'')+'"><div class="ocards">'+h.cards.map(function(c,ci){return '<span data-c="'+c.r+','+c.s+','+c.id+'"'+(h.doubled&&ci===2?' data-d="1"':'')+'></span>';}).join('')+'</div>'+
          '<div class="ometa"><span class="ot'+(x2.t>21?' bust':'')+(bj?' bj':'')+(h.result?' r'+h.result.cls:'')+'">'+tt+'</span>'+(h.result?'<span class="ores '+h.result.cls+'">'+esc(h.result.txt.split(' +')[0])+'</span>':'')+'</div></div>';}).join('')+
        (turn&&x.bot?'<span class="think"><i></i><i></i><i></i></span>':'');
      var els=[];[].forEach.call(p.ohs.querySelectorAll('[data-c]'),function(sp){var q=sp.getAttribute('data-c').split(','),el=makeCard({r:+q[0],s:q[1],id:+q[2]},false);if(sp.getAttribute('data-d')){el.classList.add('dbl');el.style.transform='rotate(90deg)';}sp.replaceWith(el);els.push(el);});
      if(n===p.n+1&&els.length&&st.phase!=='bet')flyFromShoe(els[els.length-1]);
      p.n=n;}
    if(x.say&&x.say.seq!==p.talkSeq){var fresh=p.talkSeq!==-1||Date.now()-rcvAt<50;p.talkSeq=x.say.seq;
      if(TALK&&fresh&&p.sig){p.talk.textContent=String(x.say.t||'').slice(0,90);p.talk.classList.remove('on');void p.talk.offsetWidth;p.talk.classList.add('on');clearTimeout(p.tt);p.tt=setTimeout(function(){p.talk.classList.remove('on');},3200);}}
    else if(p.talkSeq===-1)p.talkSeq=x.say?x.say.seq:0;
  }
  function msgFor(st){
    var mid=me().id,meIdx=-1,mine=null;st.seats.forEach(function(s,i){if(s.id===mid){meIdx=i;mine=s;}});
    var left=Math.ceil(Math.max(0,(st.ms||0)-(Date.now()-rcvAt))/1000);
    if(!mine)return 'Taking a seat at '+esc(st.hostName)+'\u2019s table at the next hand\u2026';
    if(st.phase==='bet'){
      BigTimer.set((st.readyMs||0)-(Date.now()-rcvAt),st.seats.filter(function(x){return x.ready;}).length+' of '+st.seats.length+' players ready');
      var oth=(Array.isArray(st.others)?st.others:[]).filter(function(o){return o.id!==mid;}).map(function(o){return esc(o.name)+' <span style="color:'+(o.net>0?'#34d399':o.net<0?'#fca5a5':'inherit')+'">'+(o.net>0?'+':'')+money(o.net)+'</span>';}).join(' &middot; ');
      if(mine.note)return mine.note+(oth?'<br><span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted)">'+oth+'</span>':'');
      if(st.solo)return bet?'Bet '+money(bet)+(sideTotal()?' + '+money(sideTotal())+' in side bets':'')+'. Press <b>Deal</b> or adjust with chips.':'Place your bet to start. Minimum '+money(R.min)+', no maximum.';
      var rl=Math.ceil(Math.max(0,(st.readyMs||0)-(Date.now()-rcvAt))/1000);
      if(myReady)return 'You are <b>ready</b>. '+(rl?'Dealing in '+rl+'s\u2026':'Waiting for the others\u2026');
      return bet?'Bet '+money(bet)+(sideTotal()?' + '+money(sideTotal())+' in side bets':'')+'. Press <b>Ready</b> when you are set.'+(rl?' Dealing in '+rl+'s.':''):'Place your bet, then press <b>Ready</b>. Minimum '+money(R.min)+'.';
    }
    BigTimer.set(0);
    if(st.phase==='deal')return 'Dealing\u2026';
    var secs=st.solo?'':' ('+left+'s)';
    if(st.phase==='insurance'){if(mine.inRound&&!mine.insDecided)return 'Dealer shows an ace. Insurance costs <b>'+money(mine.hands[0]?mine.hands[0].bet/2:0)+'</b> and pays 2 to 1.'+secs;return 'Waiting for insurance decisions\u2026'+secs;}
    if(st.phase==='peek')return 'Dealer checks for blackjack\u2026';
    if(st.phase==='play'&&st.cur){
      if(st.cur.seat===meIdx){var h=mine.hands[st.cur.hand];return (mine.hands.length>1?'Hand '+(st.cur.hand+1)+' of '+mine.hands.length+': ':'')+'You have <b>'+totalText(h.cards)+'</b>. Hit or stand?'+(st.solo?'':' <span style="color:var(--muted)">('+left+'s)</span>');}
      var who=st.seats[st.cur.seat];if(who&&who.bot)return '<b>'+esc(who.name)+'</b> is playing\u2026';return 'Waiting for <b>'+esc(who?who.name:'player')+'</b>'+secs;}
    if(st.phase==='dealer')return 'Dealer plays\u2026';
    return '';
  }
  function buttons(){
    var st=lastState;if(!st)return;
    var mid=me().id,meIdx=-1,mine=null;st.seats.forEach(function(s,i){if(s.id===mid){meIdx=i;mine=s;}});
    var bp=st.phase==='bet'&&!!mine,ip=st.phase==='insurance'&&mine&&mine.inRound&&!mine.insDecided;
    $('actBet').classList.toggle('hide',!bp);$('actIns').classList.toggle('hide',!ip);$('actPlay').classList.toggle('hide',bp||ip||!mine);
    var myTurn=st.cur&&st.cur.seat===meIdx&&st.phase==='play',lg=myTurn?st.cur.legal:{};
    $('hitBtn').disabled=!myTurn;$('standBtn').disabled=!myTurn;$('hintBtn').disabled=!myTurn;
    $('doubleBtn').disabled=!lg.double;$('splitBtn').disabled=!lg.split;$('surBtn').disabled=!lg.surrender;$('surBtn').classList.toggle('hide',!R.surrender);
    var broke=mine&&mine.bank<R.min&&bet===0;
    $('rebuyBtn').classList.toggle('hide',!broke);$('dealBtn').classList.toggle('hide',!!broke);
    $('dealBtn').disabled=!myReady&&bet<R.min;$('dealBtn').innerHTML=st.solo?'Deal &rarr;':myReady?'Not ready':'Ready &rarr;';
    setTimeout(function(){Odds.update();},0);
    var free=bank-bet-sideTotal(),lock=myReady||!bp;
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.chip'),function(c){var v=+c.getAttribute('data-v');c.disabled=lock||free<v;});
    $('allInBtn').disabled=lock||free<=0;$('dblBetBtn').disabled=lock||bet===0||bank<(bet+sideTotal())*2;$('clearBtn').disabled=lock||(bet===0&&sideTotal()===0);
    var m=msgFor(st);msg(m,mine&&st.phase==='bet'&&mine.note&&mine.noteBad);
  }
  function tick(){if(on&&lastState&&(lastState.ms||lastState.readyMs))buttons();}

  /* ===== local input while at a live table ===== */
  function submitBet(){
    var sides={};for(var k in sideBets)if(sideBets[k]>0&&activeSides.indexOf(k)>=0)sides[k]=sideBets[k];
    if(host){var s=seatOf(me().id);if(!s)return;s.bet=bet;s.sides=sides;s.ready=myReady&&bet>=R.min;if(s.ready&&!soloish())startCountdown();maybeDeal();dirty();}
    else if(L)L.send('bet',{bet:bet,sides:sides,ready:myReady});
  }
  function ready(){
    if(!lastState||lastState.phase!=='bet')return;
    if(!myReady&&bet<R.min){msg('Minimum bet is <b>'+money(R.min)+'</b>.',true);return;}
    var mine=lastState.seats.filter(function(s){return s.id===me().id;})[0];if(!mine)return;
    if(!myReady&&mine.bank<bet+sideTotal()){msg('Not enough in your bankroll for that bet.',true);return;}
    myReady=!myReady;busy=myReady;submitBet();buttons();
  }
  function localAct(kind){if(host){var s=T&&T.cur?T.seats[T.cur.seat]:null;if(s&&s.id===me().id)resolveWait(kind);}else if(L)L.send('act',{k:kind});}
  function localIns(yes){
    if(host){var s=seatOf(me().id);if(s&&s.inRound&&!s.insDecided)applyIns(s,yes);}
    else if(L)L.send('ins',{yes:!!yes});
  }
  function applyIns(s,yes){if(yes){var c=s.hands[0].bet/2;if(s.bank>=c){s.bank-=c;s.insurance=c;s.committed+=c;}}s.insDecided=true;dirty();checkIns();}
  function rebuy(){if(host){var s=seatOf(me().id);if(s){s.bank+=1000;dirty();}}else if(L)L.send('rebuy',{});start+=1000;toast('Rebought $1,000');}

  /* ===== viewer: the shuffle as the host does it ===== */
  function showViewShuf(t,sub){
    var ov=$('shuf');viewShuf=true;ov.classList.remove('see');ov.classList.add('on');$('shufSkip').style.display='none';$('shufCtl').innerHTML='';
    if(t==='Your cut'){t=lastState?esc(lastState.hostName)+' is cutting the deck':'Cutting the deck';sub='';}
    $('shufTitle').textContent=t;$('shufSub').innerHTML=sub||'';
    var stg=$('shufStage');if(!stg.querySelector('.vstack')){stg.innerHTML='';var w=document.createElement('div');w.className='vstack';for(var i=0;i<6;i++){var el=makeCard({s:'s',r:2,id:0},true);el.removeAttribute('data-id');el.style.setProperty('--i',i);w.appendChild(el);}stg.appendChild(w);}
  }
  function hideViewShuf(){if(!viewShuf)return;viewShuf=false;var ov=$('shuf');ov.classList.remove('on','see');$('shufSkip').style.display='';$('shufStage').innerHTML='';}

  /* ===== rules belong to the host while you sit at their table ===== */
  function lockRules(lock){
    document.querySelectorAll('#presets .preset,#optGame button,#optDecks button,#optS17 button,#optPay button,#optDbl button,#optDas button,#optSur button,#optHole button,#optShuf button,.sbet .tg').forEach(function(b){b.disabled=!!lock;});
    var n=$('ruleLock');if(n){n.classList.toggle('hide',!lock);n.textContent=lock?'Rules, side bets and shuffle are set by '+hostName()+', the host.':'';}
  }

  /* ===== live panel ===== */
  function liveUI(){
    var s=L?L.status:'off',dot=$('liveDot'),txt=$('liveTxt');if(!dot)return;

    try{if(window.__bjChat)window.__bjChat.refresh();}catch(e){}botWho();
    $('livePeople').innerHTML=roster.length?roster.map(function(p){return '<span class="lp'+(p.isMe?' me':'')+'">'+esc(p.name)+(p.isHost?' <i>host</i>':'')+(p.isMe?' <i>you</i>':'')+'</span>';}).join(''):'<span class="lp">Nobody else here right now.     if(document.activeElement!==$('liveName'))$('liveName').value=L?me().name:'';
  }

  /* ===== wiring ===== */
  /* switching to another game in the arcade stands you up: the hand in progress plays out without you (you stand), then the table closes */
  var away=false;
  addEventListener('message',function(e){var d=e.data;if(!d||d.arcade!=='visible')return;
    if(d.on===false&&!away){away=true;if(on&&host&&T){resolveWait('stand');var ms=seatOf(me().id);if(ms&&ms.inRound&&!ms.insDecided)applyIns(ms,false);}decide();}
    else if(d.on===true&&away){away=false;setTimeout(function(){decide();if(!on)msg('Welcome back. Place your bet to sit down.');},250);}});
  function joinView(){
    on=true;host=false;myReady=false;T=null;running=false;clearInterval(beatT);$('felt').classList.add('mp');$('dealerCards').innerHTML='';$('hands').innerHTML='';
    $('othersL').innerHTML='';$('othersR').innerHTML='';pods={};drawn={dealer:'',mine:'',others:''};

  }
  function decide(){
    var liveWant=!!(enabled&&roster.length>=2&&L&&L.status==='live');
    wantOn=!away&&(liveWant||BOTSN>0);
    if(wantOn&&!on){
      if(phase!=='bet'||busy||(typeof Ceremony!=='undefined'&&$('shuf').classList.contains('on'))){setTimeout(decide,600);return;}
      if(!liveWant||L.isHost())enterHost();else joinView();
    }else if(!wantOn&&on){
      if(host){syncSeats();if(!running&&T&&T.phase==='bet')leaveToSolo();else setTimeout(decide,800);}
      else leaveToSolo();
    }else if(on&&host){
      if(liveWant&&!L.isHost()){if(!running&&T&&T.phase==='bet'){var mine=seatOf(me().id);if(mine)bank=mine.bank;joinView();}else setTimeout(decide,800);}
      else syncSeats();
    }else if(on&&!host&&!liveWant){leaveToSolo();if(BOTSN>0&&!away)enterHost();}
    liveUI();
  }
  function botWho(){
    var el=$('botWho');if(!el)return;var list=T&&host?T.seats.filter(isBot):(lastState&&on?lastState.seats.filter(function(x){return x.bot;}):[]);
    el.innerHTML=list.length?list.map(function(x){var d=BOTBY[x.id];return d?'<div><i style="background:'+d.col+'"></i><b>'+esc(d.name)+'</b> &middot; '+esc(d.tag)+'<br><span style="font-size:11px">'+esc(d.style)+'</span></div>':'';}).join('')+
      '<div style="grid-column:1 / -1;font-size:11px">'+(BOTDEF.length-list.length)+' more regulars wait at the rail. When someone goes broke, the next one sits down.</div>':'<div style="grid-column:1 / -1">Just you and the dealer.</div>';
  }
  function init(){
    if(typeof Live!=='undefined'){
      L=Live.create({game:'blackjack',maxSeats:MAXS});myId=L.me.id;

      L.on('roster',function(list){roster=list;decide();});
      L.on('host',function(isH){
        if(isH&&on&&!host){var st=lastState;hideViewShuf();enterHost(st);}
        else if(!isH&&host&&on&&roster.length>=2){if(!running&&T&&T.phase==='bet'){var mine=seatOf(me().id);if(mine)bank=mine.bank;joinView();}}
        decide();
      });
      L.on('msg:state',function(st){if(!on||host)return;render(st);});

      L.on('msg:shufdone',function(){if(on&&!host)hideViewShuf();});
      L.on('msg:hello',function(p,from){if(on&&host)cast();});
      L.on('msg:bet',function(p,from){if(!on||!host||!T||T.phase!=='bet'||running)return;var s=seatOf(from);if(!s)return;
        var b=Math.max(0,+p.bet||0),sides={},st=0;for(var k in (p.sides||{})){var a=Math.max(0,+p.sides[k]||0);if(a>0&&activeSides.indexOf(k)>=0){sides[k]=a;st+=a;}}
        if(b+st>s.bank){b=0;sides={};}s.bet=b;s.sides=sides;s.ready=!!p.ready&&b>=R.min;if(s.ready)startCountdown();maybeDeal();dirty();});
      L.on('msg:act',function(p,from){if(!on||!host||!T||!T.cur)return;var s=T.seats[T.cur.seat];if(s&&s.id===from&&['hit','stand','double','split','surrender'].indexOf(p.k)>=0)resolveWait(p.k);});

      L.on('msg:rebuy',function(p,from){if(!on||!host||!T)return;var s=seatOf(from);if(s&&s.bank<R.min*4){s.bank+=1000;dirty();}});
      $('liveName').addEventListener('change',function(){L.setName(this.value);liveUI();if(on&&host){var s=seatOf(me().id);if(s){s.name=me().name;dirty();}}});
      seg('optLive',function(v){enabled=v==='1';saveSettings();if(enabled){L.start();}else{L.stop();roster=[];}decide();});
    }
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#optBots button'),function(x){x.classList.toggle('on',+x.getAttribute('data-v')===BOTSN);});
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#optTalk button'),function(x){x.classList.toggle('on',x.getAttribute('data-v')===(TALK?'1':'0'));});

    seg('optTalk',function(v){TALK=v==='1';saveSettings();if(!TALK)for(var k in pods)pods[k].talk.classList.remove('on');});
    tickT=setInterval(tick,500);
    if(L&&enabled)L.start();
    decide();liveUI();
  }
  return {
    wGet:function(){if(on&&host&&T){var m=seatOf(me().id);return m?m.bank:bank;}return bank;},
    wSet:function(x){if(on&&host&&T){var m=seatOf(me().id);if(m){m.bank=x;dirty();}}bank=x;},

    enabled:function(){return enabled;},
    capHook:function(t,sub){if(on&&host&&L&&roster.length>=2)L.send('shuf',{t:t,sub:sub});},
    committed:function(){var st=lastState;if(!st)return 0;var m=st.seats.filter(function(s){return s.id===me().id;})[0];return m?m.committed:0;},
    dbg:{state:function(){return lastState;},T:function(){return T;},roster:function(){return roster;},me:function(){return me();},host:function(){return host;},on:function(){return on;},
      act:function(k){localAct(k);},noFirstShuffle:function(){firstShoe=false;},ready:function(){ready();},ins:function(y){localIns(y);},setTimeouts:function(turn,ins,rdy){TURN_MS=turn;INS_MS=ins;READY_MS=rdy;}}};

<\u002fscript>
<script>
(function(){
  var last=0;
  var sent=document.createElement('div');
  sent.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  sent.style.cssText='height:0;margin:0;padding:0;border:0;clear:both';
  function measure(){
    if(!document.body) return 0;
    if(sent.parentNode!==document.body) document.body.appendChild(sent);
    var pb=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.body).paddingBottom)||0;
    return Math.ceil(sent.getBoundingClientRect().bottom+(window.pageYOffset||0)+pb);
  }
  function post(){
    var h=measure();
    if(!h||Math.abs(h-last)<2) return;
    last=h;
    try{parent.postMessage({arcade:'h',h:h},'*');}catch(e){}
  }
  addEventListener('load',post); addEventListener('resize',post);
  if(window.ResizeObserver){
    try{ var ro=new ResizeObserver(post); ro.observe(document.body);
         var w=document.querySelector('.wrap'); if(w) ro.observe(w); }catch(e){}
  }
  document.addEventListener('load',post,true);
  addEventListener('message',function(e){if(e.data&&e.data.arcade==='visible'&&e.data.on){last=0;setTimeout(post,30);}});
  setInterval(post,900); post();
})();
